Let's design a practical code with several layers:
1. Fast evaluator using FFT and direct for small.
2. Candidate generators:
   - Uniform (baseline)
   - Alternating two-level with optimized r and starting phase, maybe prime-period patterns (low-discrepancy)
   - Random smooth masks (low-pass filtered Bernoulli)
   - Random Gaussian low frequencies (weights)
   - Analytic nonlinear: sorted by sine / Fourier series.
3. Improvers:
   a. Additive coordinate projected gradient / multiplicative weights with subgradient active lags.
   b. "Nelder-Mead" over low-dimensional Fourier parameters.
   c. Local search / simulated annealing on coordinates for smaller n (maybe 2048) then upscale? Could be too slow.
4. Time management: evaluate many quick candidates with internal objective; keep top 8. Spend most time improving top candidates with multiplicative/subgradient.

Need ensure final valid list. Since we can't know search results, robust fallback: if no score < some threshold, return best from heuristic even if 2. But to maximize reward, likely heuristics find under target.

Let's refine algorithm components.

Fast objective:
```
def norm_conv_sq(p):
    n=p.size
    # p nonnegative? clip
    S=p.sum()
    if S <= small: return inf, p, None
    p=p/S
    # FFT length M >= 2n-1, choose next power of two maybe
    M = 1 << (2*n-1).bit_length()
    F=np.fft.rfft(p,M)
    b=np.fft.irfft(F*F,M)[:2*n-1]
    b=np.maximum(b,0)
    return 2*n*b.max(), p, b
```
Normalization p sum1. Multiplication F*F (complex? p real, F complex, square not abs; correct for convolution not autocorr. Use F*F not |F|^2.)

```
def mult_update(p, iters, cap_factor=0.95, target=None):
   p=normalize(p)
   n=p.size; L=2*n-1; M=next_pow2(3*n)
   P=fft(p,M)
   for...
      b=ifft(P*P,M)[:L]; b.clip
      maxb=b.max()
      if target is not None: level=target else level=maxb*cap_factor
      T=np.minimum(b, max(level,1e-12)); sT=T.sum(); if sT<=0: T=uniform
      T/=sT
      PF=fft(p,M)
      TF? We can precompute FFT of T each iter (changes). T length L zero pad M. Conv = ifft(PF*TF,M)
      N=conv[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
      # B conv for D: BF=fft(b,M) (b changes); D=ifft(PF*BF,M)...
      D=...
      ratio=N/(D+1e-15)
      p=p*ratio; p=normalize(p)
      # sometimes evaluate
   return p
```

```
def subgrad_update(p, iters, deadline):
   best=p.copy(); best_score=fastscore(p)
   for it ...:
      score,b,flo = fastscore(p)
      # top active
      maxb=b.max()
      if maxb<=0: break
      active=np.where(b >= maxb - 0.1*maxb)[0]
      if len(active)>512: active=b.argsort()[-512:]
      g=np.zeros(n)
      for k in active:
         lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
         if lo<=hi:
            idx=np.arange(lo,hi+1)
            src=k-idx[::-1]? Wait need g_i=2 p_{k-i}. If idx lo..hi, source indices k-idx in descending. slice p[k-hi : k-lo?]. Use reversed: g[lo:hi+1] += 2 * p[k-hi:k-lo+1][::-1]
      gmean=np.mean(g)
```

Implement:
```
OBJ_N = 0
OBJ_TAU = 1e-4
OBJ_USE_W = True

def _lbgf_obj_grad(z):
    n=OBJ_N; tau=OBJ_TAU
    p = softmax(z)  # stable
    # compute b via FFT length n
    M = 1 << (2*n -1).bit_length()
    F = np.fft.rfft(p, M)
    b = np.fft.irfft(F*F, M)[:2*n-1]
    b = np.maximum(b,0)
    maxb = b.max()
    if maxb <=1e-300: return 0, empty
    # weights for smooth max: to avoid memory, compute shifted=(b-maxb)/tau; use max shift? shift already <=0.
    # If tau too small, exp underflows; only compute for b > maxb - tau*20
    shift = (b - maxb)/tau
    # w = exp(shift - logsumexp); logsum = maxshift(0) + log(mean(exp(shift)))? Use stable.
    logmean = logsumexp(shift) (since max 0). Implement manually: mask = shift > -20; exps=np.exp(shift[mask]); logsum= np.log(exps.sum()); w_mask=exp/logsum; full w zeros.
    if mask count small? maybe many. For full gradient, need w length. Could assign w=zeros, w[mask]=...
    # If tau large, many active; okay length L~8191.
    # compute v via correlation w and p. M2 >= 3n, next pow2.
    M2 = 1 << (3*n -1).bit_length()
    W = np.fft.fft(w, M2)
    P = np.fft.fft(p, M2)
    corr = np.fft.ifft(W * np.conj(P)).real[:n]
    v=2*corr
    dot=np.dot(p,v)
    grad=p*(v-dot)
    val= maxb + tau*logmean  # smooth max
    return val, grad
```

```
def _lbfgs_fun(x):
    n = OBJ_N
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    z[:-1] = x
    z[-1] = 0.0
    # recenter for numerical? subtract mean maybe but then last not 0; softmax invariant. We can subtract z.max() not mean.
    zm = z - z.max()
    e = np.exp(zm)
    p = e / e.sum()
    M1 = 1 << (2*n - 1).bit_length()
    F = np.fft.rfft(p, M1)
    b = np.fft.irfft(F*F, M1)[:2*n-1]
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    maxb = float(b.max())
    tau = OBJ_TAU
    if maxb <= 1e-300:
        val=0.0; grad=np.zeros_like(x); return val, grad
    shift = (b - maxb) / tau
    # To avoid exp of very small, mask > -50
    mask = shift > -50.0
    exps = np.exp(shift[mask])
    expsum = float(exps.sum())
    if expsum <= 0: # fallback all weights to argmax
       w = np.zeros(2*n-1); w[int(np.argmax(b))]=1.0; logmean=0.0
    else:
       logsum = np.log(expsum) # since max shift 0, includes at least 1
       logmean = logsum - np.log(2*n-1)
       w = np.zeros(2*n-1)
       w[mask]=exps/expsum
    smooth = maxb + tau*logmean
    # correlation
    M2 = 1 << (3*n - 2).bit_length()
    # Could use rfft for real: W = np.fft.fft(w, M2); P = np.fft.fft(p, M2)
    W = np.fft.fft(w, M2)
    P = np.fft.fft(p, M2)
    corr = np.fft.ifft(W * np.conj(P)).real[:n]
    v = 2.0 * corr
    dot = float(np.dot(p, v))
    grad_z = p * (v - dot)
    # gradient wrt x length n-1 = first n-1 components of grad_z
    grad = grad_z[:-1] * (2.0 * n)
    val = smooth * (2.0 * n)
    return val, grad
```
Check derivative and factor: val=2n*smooth, grad=2n*grad_z. Good.


  score,b = fast_score(p)
  maxb=b.max()

  g zeros; for k in active: slice; g[lo:hi+1] += 2*p[k-hi:k-lo+1][::-1]

```
def make_base_candidates(n, rng):
    cands=[]
    xg=np.arange(n)/max(1,n-1)
    cands.append(np.ones(n)/n)
    # monotone exp
    for a in (-2.0,-1.0,-0.5,0.5,1.0,2.0):
        p=np.exp(a*xg); cands.append(p/p.sum())
    # two-step
    for eps in (0.1,0.25,0.3333,0.5,0.7):
       p=np.ones(n); p[:n//2]+=eps; p[n//2:]-=eps; p=max(p,0); if sum>0 append
       p2 swap
    # alternating two-level
    for r in (0.2,0.5,0.8,1.2,1.5,2.0,4.0):
       p=np.ones(n); p[1::2]=r; append; p[0::2]=r append
    # random smooth
    for trial in range(8):
       vals=np.zeros(n)
       for m in [1,2,3,5,8,13, n//8,n//4]: # but n//8 float okay
          f=2*math.pi*m/n? if m int. Use np.arange(n)*m? For m large, okay.
          phase=rng.uniform(0,2π)
          amp=0.25/(math.sqrt(m)+1)
          vals += amp*(rng.normal()*np.cos(m*np.arange(n)*2π/n+phase)+...)
       # also add alternating amp 0.15
       vals += 0.15*np.cos(π*np.arange(n))
       p=np.exp(vals); normalize
    # sinusoid parameter random
    for trial in range(8):
       p=1 + a*np.sin(2πxg+φ)+ b*np.cos(4πxg+ψ)+ c*np.cos(πxg)+ d*(xg-0.5)
       clip, normalize
    return cands
```

Detailed functions:
```
def _softmax_x(x, n):
    z = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    z[:-1]=x
    z[-1]=0.0
    z -= np.max(z)
    e=np.exp(z)
    s=e.sum()
    if s <=0 or not finite: return np.full(n,1.0/n)
    return e/s

def _p_to_x(p):
    p=np.asarray(p)
    z=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300))
    x=z[:-1]-z[-1]
    return np.clip(x, -25, 25)
```

Now, candidate exact internal score `fast_score(p)`:
```
def fast_score(p):
    p = np.asarray(p,float)
    s=p.sum()
    if s <=0: return inf, None
    p=p/s
    n=p.size
    M=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    F=np.fft.rfft(p,M)
    b=np.fft.irfft(F*F,M)[:2*n-1]
    b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
    sc=2.0*n*float(b.max())
    return sc,b
```
If p has tiny negative from numerical, clip.

When evaluating actual, careful: `evaluate_sequence` expects list; if p contains negative tiny, clip. Convert.
```
def safe_to_list(p):
   p=np.asarray(p,float); p=np.maximum(p,0); s=p.sum(); if s<1e-12: p=np.ones_like(p)/len; else p/=s
   p=np.clip(p,0,1000)
   return [float(v) for v in p]
```
Maybe if p has len huge >? okay.

`run_lbfgs_continuation` implementation:
```
def run_lbfgs_continuation(p, n, budget_s, rng):
    p = normalize(p)
    best_p = p.copy(); best_score,_ = fast_score(p)
    t0 = time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+budget_s
    scale = 1.0/n
    fracs = [0.25,0.15,0.08,0.04,0.02,0.01]
    for frac in fracs:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline-10: break
        tau = frac * scale
        # If tau too small relative to current max? maybe tau = min(frac*scale, 0.2*maxb?)
        _set_obj(n, tau)
        x = _p_to_x(best_p)
        # random jitter to escape plateau
        x = x + rng.normal(0, 0.01, size=x.size)
        maxiter = 100
        if n > 4000: maxiter = 80
        try:
            res = spopt.minimize(_lbfgs_fun, x, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=(-25,25), options={'maxiter':maxiter, 'ftol':1e-8, 'gtol':1e-7, 'maxls':20})
        except Exception:
            break
        cand = _softmax_x(res.x, n)
        sc,_ = fast_score(cand)
        if sc < best_score:
            best_score=sc; best_p=cand.copy()
        # Evaluate also maybe at intermediate - not available.
    # peak refine
    remaining=max(0, deadline-time.monotonic())
    if remaining>5:
       best_p = peak_refine(best_p, remaining*0.7)
       sc,_=fast_score(best_p); best_score=sc
    return best_p
```

Now, `peak_refine` function. Need not use nested. We'll implement:
```
def peak_refine(p, budget_s):
    p=normalize(p); best=p.copy(); score,b=fast_score(p); t0; deadline
    if score inf: return best
    eta=0.5  # multiplier in factor?
    for it in range(20000):
       if time>=deadline: break
       # recompute if p changed
       score,b=fast_score(p) # could cache but ok
       maxb=b.max()
       if maxb<=0: break
       active=np.flatnonzero(b >= maxb - 0.03*maxb)
       if active.size==0: active=np.argmax(b) # flatnonzero returns sup? set
       if active.size>128:
           active = active[np.argsort(-b[active])[:128]]
       g=np.zeros(p.size)
       n=p.size
       for k in active:
          lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
          if lo<=hi:
             g[lo:hi+1] += 2.0 * p[k-hi:k-lo+1][::-1]
       gmax=np.max(g); gm=np.mean(g)
       if gmax <= 1e-300: break
       # choose scale to keep max ratio 0.6: delta= log(1/0.6)
       scale = (np.log(1.0/0.6)) / (gmax - gm) if gmax>gm else 0.5
       # shift = -alpha*(g-gm); at max positive alpha*(gmax-gm) -> وا? If gmax>gm, shift negative reduce high; low g<gm shift positive increase.
       shift = -scale*(g-gm)
       shift = np.clip(shift, -4, 4)
       newp = p*np.exp(shift)
       newp=normalize(newp)
       sc,_=fast_score(newp)
       if sc < score:
          p=newp; best=newp.copy(); score=sc
          # increase scale slightly
          scale *= 1.05
       else:
          # try half
          shift2 = shift*0.5
          newp=normalize(p*np.exp(np.clip(shift2,-4,4)))
          sc2,_=fast_score(newp)
          if sc2 < score:
             p=newp; best=newp.copy(); score=sc2
          else:
             scale *= 0.7
             if scale < 1e-4: break
    return best
```
Need careful: scale variable defined as multiplier; we use shift. Recompute scale each iter from current g maybe simpler: `scale = 0.25 / max(np.max(np.abs(g-gm)),1e-300)` gives max ratio e^0.25≈1.28. Use fixed.
```
dmax=np.max(np.abs(g-gm)); if dmax<... break; shift=np.clip(-0.25*(g-gm)/dmax? If divide, max shift 0.25. Use shift = np.clip(-0.25*(g-gm)/dmax, -2,2). This ensures max change 1.28. If worsening, reduce factor.
```
Update factor can be stored `step=1.0`, shift=-step*(g-gm)/dmax.
If no improvement, step*=0.7.
This is okay.

However active set based on max b; if p changes, recompute. Good.

This peak_refine is similar to subgradient multiplicative but normalized by dmax; may improve.

Now, let's refine the L-BFGS objective to avoid performance overhead of full `np.exp(shift)` over L where tau very small and shift large negative (underflow warnings). Use mask:
```
shift = (b - maxb) / tau
mask = shift > -40.0
exps_full = np.zeros(L); exps_full[mask]=np.exp(shift[mask])
expsum = exps_full.sum()
if expsum <=0: w index argmax
else:
  logsum = np.log(expsum)
  w = np.zeros(L); w[mask]=exps_full[mask]/expsum
```
This is faster and avoids underflow. If tau small, few active. But logmean excludes negligible; okay. For gradient, weights normalized. Use `logsum - logL`.

```
def resample_seq(p,n):
   p = np.asarray(p,float)
   p = normalize_seq(p)
   m=p.size
   if m==n: return p
   x_new=np.linspace(0,m-1,n)
   y=np.interp(x_new,np.arange(m),p)
   return normalize_seq(y)
```

```
def make_candidates(n, rng):
    cands=[]
    arr=np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t=arr/(n-1) if n>1 else np.array([0.0])
    push_candidate(cands, np.ones(n))
    # ramps
    p=arr+1.0; push; p=p[::-1]; push; p=1.0 - t; push? exp
    for a in (-2.0,-1.5,-1.0,-0.5,0.5,1.0,1.5,2.0):
       p=np.exp(a*t); push
    # two-step
    for eps in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.333333,0.5,0.7,0.9):
       p=np.ones(n); p[:n//2]+=eps; p[n//2:]-=eps; push
       p=np.ones(n); p[:n//2]-=eps; p[n//2:]+=eps; push
    # alternating
    for r in (0.1,0.2,0.4,0.6,0.8,1.2,1.5,2.0,3.0,5.0):
       p=np.ones(n); p[1::2]=r; push
       p=np.ones(n); p[0::2]=r; push
    # random smooth
    for _ in range(12):
       vals=np.zeros(n)
       for m in (1,2,3,5,8,13,21,34):
          amp=0.20/math.sqrt(float(m)+1.0)
          phase=rng.uniform(0.0,2.0*math.pi)
          phase2=phase + rng.uniform(0,2π)
          vals += amp*(rng.normal()*np.cos(2π*m*arr/n + phase) + rng.normal()*np.sin(...))
       vals += 0.15*np.cos(math.pi*arr)
       p=np.exp(np.clip(vals, -3,3)); push
    # low-dim deterministic wavy
    for a in (-0.6,-0.3,0,0.3,0.6): for b maybe
       p=1.0 + a*np.sin(2π*t) + b*np.cos(4π*t) + c*np.cos(π*t) + d*(t-0.5)
```

```
def multiplicative_update(p, iters, cap_factor=0.95):
   p=normalize; best=p; bestsc=fastscore
   for it in range(iters):
      sc,b=fastscore(p); maxb=b.max(); if maxb<=0 break
      cap=maxb*cap_factor
      T=np.minimum(b, cap); sT=T.sum(); if sT<=0: T=np.full_like(b,1)
      T=T/sT
      M2=next_pow2(3*n)
      P=np.fft.fft(p,M2)
      N=np.fft.ifft(P*np.fft.fft(T,M2)).real
      N=N[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
      B=np.fft.fft(b,M2) # b length L zero pad
      D=np.fft.ifft(P*B).real
      D=D[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
      ratio=N/(D+1e-30)
      ratio=np.clip(ratio,0.01,100)
      newp=normalize(p*ratio)
      sc2=fastscore(newp)
      if sc2<bestsc: ...
      if no improvement for 10: break
```
But computing `np.fft.fft(b,M2)` each iter; okay. This NMF update may make p non-convergent; cap_factor 0.98. We can use if no SciPy.

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    start=time.monotonic()
    deadline=start+max(10, budget_s - 90)

       for _ in range(3):
          p2=run_lbfgs_continuation(best_p, n, min(180, deadline-now-120), rng)
          sc2,_=fast_score(p2); update; if sc2 not improved break
    # Sort answers by internal; include best even if missing
    if best_p is not None: answers.append((best_score,best_p))
    answers.sort(key=tuple_key0)
    final_seq=None; best_actual=inf
    for sc,p in answers[:5]:
        seq=safe_to_list(p)
        val=get_actual_score(seq)
        if val<best_actual: best_actual=val; final_seq=seq
        if val < 1.5: break
    if final_seq is None:
        if best_p is not None: final_seq=safe_to_list(best_p)
        else: final_seq=[1.0]
    return final_seq
```

```python
"""
Search for low-peak nonnegative convolution sequences.

This routine uses a combination of:
  * analytic candidate templates (ramps, two-level blocks, alternating patterns,
    random smooth/aliasing perturbations),
  * small-length template optimization to obtain scaled starting shapes,
  * full-length L-BFGS on a softmax parameterization with a smoothed-max
    surrogate for the convolution peak,
  * a deterministic multiplicative subgradient refinement of the exact max peak.

The final answer is chosen by calling the supplied evaluate_sequence() on the
best internal-score candidates.
"""

import time
import math
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy import optimize as spopt
    HAVE_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    HAVE_SCIPY = False

# Globals used by the top-level L-BFGS objective.  No closures/lambda allowed.
OBJ_N = 0
OBJ_TAU = 1e-6
OBJ_M1 = 1
OBJ_M2 = 1
OBJ_LOGL = 0.0


def next_pow2(m):
    """Smallest power of two >= m (m positive integer-like)."""
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    m = int(m)
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_seq(p):
    """Clip to nonnegative finite values and normalize sum to 1."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    if p.ndim != 1 or p.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        n = p.size
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    return p / s


def resample_seq(p, n):
    """Linearly resample a nonnegative sequence to length n."""
    p = normalize_seq(p)
    m = p.size
    if m == n:
        return p
    if m <= 0 or n <= 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(max(1, n))
    x_old = np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, float(m - 1), int(n), dtype=np.float64)
    y = np.interp(x_new, x_old, p)
    return normalize_seq(y)


def fast_score(p):
    """
    Fast FFT evaluation of the objective (sum normalized to 1).
    Returns (score, b) where b is the convolution sequence.
    """
    p = normalize_seq(p)
    n = p.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    M = next_pow2(L)
    F = np.fft.rfft(p, M)
    b = np.fft.irfft(F * F, M)[:L]
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    mx = float(b.max()) if b.size else 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * mx), b


def push_candidate(cands, p):
    """Append a sanitized normalized candidate to cands if valid."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    if p.ndim != 1 or p.size == 0:
        return
    p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if np.isfinite(s) and s > 1e-300:
        cands.append(p / s)


def tuple_key0(item):
    return item[0]


def _set_obj(n, tau):
    global OBJ_N, OBJ_TAU, OBJ_M1, OBJ_M2, OBJ_LOGL
    OBJ_N = int(n)
    OBJ_TAU = float(max(1e-14, tau))
    L = 2 * OBJ_N - 1
    OBJ_M1 = next_pow2(L)
    # Need enough length for correlation of w (2n-1) and p (n).
    OBJ_M2 = next_pow2(3 * OBJ_N + 1)
    OBJ_LOGL = math.log(float(L))


def _softmax_x(x, n):
    """
    Softmax with the last coordinate fixed to 0.
    x length n-1.
    """
    z = np.empty(int(n), dtype=np.float64)
    z[:-1] = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    z[-1] = 0.0
    z -= float(np.max(z))
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(int(n), 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def _p_to_x(p):
    """Convert p to softmax parameterization with last log-value fixed to 0."""
    p = normalize_seq(p)
    n = p.size
    if n == 1:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    x = z[:-1] - z[-1]
    return np.nan_to_num(np.clip(x, -25.0, 25.0), nan=0.0, posinf=25.0, neginf=-25.0)


def _lbfgs_fun(x):
    """
    Smoothed-max objective and gradient.

    x: length n-1 softmax parameters, p_{n-1} is anchored at log-value 0.
    Returns (value, gradient) for value = 2n * smoothed_max(p*p).
    """
    n = OBJ_N
    tau = OBJ_TAU
    M1 = OBJ_M1
    M2 = OBJ_M2
    logL = OBJ_LOGL
    L = 2 * n - 1

    p = _softmax_x(x, n)

    # Convolution b = p * p.
    F = np.fft.rfft(p, M1)
    b = np.fft.irfft(F * F, M1)[:L]
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    maxb = float(b.max())

    if maxb <= 1e-300:
        return 0.0, np.zeros_like(x, dtype=np.float64)

    shift = (b - maxb) / tau

    # Softmax weights over b, ignoring negligible entries.
    mask = shift > -40.0
    exps = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    with np.errstate(under="ignore", over="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
        exps[mask] = np.exp(shift[mask])
    expsum = float(exps.sum())

    if not np.isfinite(expsum) or expsum <= 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(b))] = 1.0
        logsum = 0.0
    else:
        w = exps / expsum
        logsum = math.log(expsum)

    # Smoothed maximum: max + tau * log(mean exp((b-max)/tau)).
    smooth = maxb + tau * (logsum - logL)

    # Gradient wrt p:
    #   d/dp_i [sum_k w_k (p*p)_k] = 2 sum_k w_k p_{k-i}
    # computed as correlation of w with p.
    W = np.fft.fft(w, M2)
    P = np.fft.fft(p, M2)
    corr = np.fft.ifft(W * np.conj(P)).real[:n]
    vp = 2.0 * corr

    # Gradient wrt softmax parameters:
    #   p_i (vp_i - sum_j p_j vp_j)
    dot = float(np.dot(p, vp))
    gradx = p[:-1] * (vp[:-1] - dot) * (2.0 * n)
    val = smooth * (2.0 * n)

    if not np.isfinite(val):
        val = 1e300
    gradx = np.nan_to_num(gradx, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return float(val), gradx


def multiplicative_update(p, budget_s, cap_factor=0.96):
    """
    Rank-one multiplicative update toward a peak-capped target.
    Used as a robust fallback/polish when exact peak derivative is hard.
    """
    p = normalize_seq(p)
    n = p.size
    best = p.copy()
    score, b = fast_score(p)
    best_score = score
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return best

    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(0.0, float(budget_s))
    M2 = next_pow2(3 * n + 1)
    improve_count = 0
    stagn = 0

    for it in range(10000):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break

        score, b = fast_score(p)
        if not np.isfinite(score):
            break
        maxb = float(b.max())
        if maxb <= 1e-300:
            break

        cap = maxb * cap_factor
        T = np.minimum(b, cap)
        sT = float(T.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(sT) or sT <= 1e-300:
            break
        T = T / sT

        P = np.fft.fft(p, M2)
        N = np.fft.ifft(P * np.fft.fft(T, M2)).real
        N = N[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]

        B = np.fft.fft(b, M2)
        D = np.fft.ifft(P * B).real
        D = D[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]

        ratio = N / (D + 1e-300)
        ratio = np.nan_to_num(ratio, nan=1.0, posinf=1.0, neginf=1.0)
        ratio = np.clip(ratio, 0.05, 20.0)

        newp = normalize_seq(p * ratio)
        sc2, _ = fast_score(newp)

        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < score - 1e-15:
            p = newp
            if sc2 < best_score - 1e-12:
                best = p.copy()
                best_score = sc2
            improve_count += 1
            stagn = 0
        else:
            stagn += 1
            if stagn > 60:
                break
            cap_factor *= 0.97
            if cap_factor < 0.60:
                break

    return best


def peak_refine(p, budget_s):
    """
    Multiplicative subgradient refinement for the exact maximum lag(s).
    """
    p = normalize_seq(p)
    n = p.size
    best = p.copy()
    score, b = fast_score(p)
    best_score = score
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return best

    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(0.0, float(budget_s))
    step = 0.35
    stagn = 0

    for it in range(60000):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break

        score, b = fast_score(p)
        if not np.isfinite(score):
            break
        maxb = float(b.max())
        if maxb <= 1e-300:
            break

        active = np.flatnonzero(b >= maxb - 0.04 * maxb)
        if active.size == 0:
            active = np.array([int(np.argmax(b))], dtype=np.int64)
        if active.size > 128:
            part = np.argpartition(b[active], -128)[-128:]
            active = active[part]

        g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for kk in active:
            k = int(kk)
            lo = k - n + 1
            if lo < 0:
                lo = 0
            hi = k
            if hi >= n:
                hi = n - 1
            if lo <= hi:
                g[lo:hi + 1] += 2.0 * p[k - hi:k - lo + 1][::-1]

        gm = float(np.mean(g))
        dmax = float(np.max(np.abs(g - gm)))
        if not np.isfinite(dmax) or dmax <= 1e-300:
            break

        shift = -step * (g - gm) / dmax
        shift = np.clip(shift, -3.0, 3.0)
        newp = normalize_seq(p * np.exp(shift))
        sc2, _ = fast_score(newp)

        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < score - 1e-15:
            p = newp
            if sc2 < best_score - 1e-12:
                best = p.copy()
                best_score = sc2
            step = min(2.0, step * 1.03)
            stagn = 0
        else:
            # Smaller half-step attempt.
            shift2 = -0.5 * step * (g - gm) / dmax
            shift2 = np.clip(shift2, -2.0, 2.0)
            newp = normalize_seq(p * np.exp(shift2))
            sc3, _ = fast_score(newp)
            if np.isfinite(sc3) and sc3 < score - 1e-15:
                p = newp
                if sc3 < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best = p.copy()
                    best_score = sc3
                step = min(2.0, step * 1.02)
                stagn = 0
            else:
                stagn += 1
                step *= 0.75
                if step < 1e-4 or stagn > 250:
                    break

    return best


def make_candidates(n, rng):
    """Generate a diversified set of normalized nonnegative candidate sequences."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return []
    cands = []
    arr = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = arr / float(max(1, n - 1))

    # Uniform and monotone shapes.
    push_candidate(cands, np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
    push_candidate(cands, arr + 1.0)
    push_candidate(cands, (arr + 1.0)[::-1])
    push_candidate(cands, np.ones(n) - 0.99 * t)

    # Exponential ramps.
    for a in (-2.0, -1.5, -1.0, -0.75, -0.5, -0.25, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0):
        push_candidate(cands, np.exp(a * t))

    # Two-block step shapes.
    for eps in (0.03, 0.07, 0.12, 0.2, 0.333333, 0.5, 0.65, 0.8):
        p1 = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        p1[: n // 2] += eps
        p1[n // 2:] -= eps
        push_candidate(cands, p1)

        p2 = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        p2[: n // 2] -= eps
        p2[n // 2:] += eps
        push_candidate(cands, p2)

    # Three-block shapes.
    for eps in (0.1, 0.25, 0.45, 0.7):
        p3 = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        third = n // 3
        p3[:third] += eps
        p3[third:2 * third] -= eps
        p3[2 * third:] += 0.5 * eps
        push_candidate(cands, p3)
        p3b = p3[::-1].copy()
        push_candidate(cands, p3b)

    # Alternating two-level shapes.
    for r in (0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.35, 0.5, 0.65, 0.8, 1.1, 1.3, 1.7, 2.5, 5.0):
        p1 = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        p1[1::2] = r
        push_candidate(cands, p1)

        p2 = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        p2[0::2] = r
        push_candidate(cands, p2)

    # Random smooth Fourier-like perturbations.
    for _ in range(16):
        vals = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        freqs = (1, 2, 3, 5, 8, 13, 21, 34, 55)
        for m in freqs:
            ang = 2.0 * math.pi * float(m) * arr / float(n)
            amp = 0.22 / math.sqrt(float(m) + 1.0)
            a1 = float(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0))
            a2 = float(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0))
            vals += amp * (a1 * np.cos(ang) + a2 * np.sin(ang))
        vals += 0.18 * float(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0)) * np.cos(math.pi * arr)
        vals = np.clip(vals, -4.0, 4.0)
        push_candidate(cands, np.exp(vals))

    # Low-dimensional random sinusoidal templates.
    for _ in range(14):
        ph1 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
        ph2 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
        ph3 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
        a1 = float(rng.uniform(-0.7, 0.7))
        a2 = float(rng.uniform(-0.45, 0.45))
        a3 = float(rng.uniform(-0.30, 0.30))
        a4 = float(rng.uniform(-0.35, 0.35))
        p = (
            1.0
            + a1 * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * t + ph1)
            + a2 * np.cos(4.0 * math.pi * t + ph2)
            + a3 * np.cos(math.pi * arr + ph3)
            + a4 * (t - 0.5)
        )
        push_candidate(cands, p)

    # Very random low-pass-ish masks, stays positive by exponentiating noise.
    for _ in range(8):
        vals = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for _inner in range(6):
            center = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0))
            width = float(rng.uniform(0.03, 0.35))
            amp = float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.55))
            vals += amp * np.exp(-((t - center) ** 2) / (2.0 * width * width))
        push_candidate(cands, np.exp(vals))

    return cands


def run_lbfgs_continuation(p, n, budget_s, rng):
    """
    Smoothed-max L-BFGS continuation from p.
    tau is gradually reduced.  If SciPy is unavailable, uses robust updates.
    """
    n = int(n)
    p = normalize_seq(p)
    best = p.copy()
    best_score, _ = fast_score(p)

    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(0.0, float(budget_s))

    if not HAVE_SCIPY:
        p = multiplicative_update(best, min(0.35 * float(budget_s), 60.0))
        sc, _ = fast_score(p)
        if sc < best_score:
            best = p.copy()
            best_score = sc
        rem = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
        if rem > 5.0:
            best = peak_refine(best, rem)
            sc, _ = fast_score(best)
            if sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
        return best

    fracs = (0.28, 0.17, 0.10, 0.06, 0.035, 0.022, 0.014)
    current = best.copy()

    for frac in fracs:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= deadline - 8.0:
            break

        sc_cur, b_cur = fast_score(current)
        if not np.isfinite(sc_cur):
            current = best
            sc_cur, b_cur = fast_score(current)
        maxb_cur = float(b_cur.max())
        scale = maxb_cur if maxb_cur > 1e-300 else (1.0 / float(n))
        tau = frac * scale
        _set_obj(n, tau)

        x0 = _p_to_x(current)
        x0 = x0 + 0.02 * rng.standard_normal(x0.size)
        x0 = np.nan_to_num(np.clip(x0, -25.0, 25.0), nan=0.0, posinf=25.0, neginf=-25.0)

        if n <= 256:
            maxiter = 350
            maxfun = 8000
        elif n <= 1024:
            maxiter = 250
            maxfun = 5000
        elif n <= 4096:
            maxiter = 180
            maxfun = 3000
        else:
            maxiter = 120
            maxfun = 1800

        options = {
            "maxiter": maxiter,
            "maxfun": maxgun := maxfun,
            "maxls": 20,
            "ftol": 1e-9,
            "gtol": 1e-8,
            "disp": False,
        }

        res = None
        try:
            res = spopt.minimize(
                _lbfgs_fun,
                x0,
                jac=True,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=(-25.0, 25.0),
                options=options,
            )
        except Exception:
            pass

        cand = None
        if res is not None:
            rx = getattr(res, "x", None)
            if rx is not None:
                rx = np.nan_to_num(np.asarray(rx, dtype=np.float64), nan=0.0, posinf=25.0, neginf=-25.0)
                cand = _softmax_x(rx, n)

        if cand is None:
            cand = current

        sc, _ = fast_score(cand)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_score = sc
                best = cand.copy()
            # Use cand as next stage start if it is not dramatically worse.
            if sc <= best_score * 1.25 + 1e-9 or np.isfinite(sc_cur) and sc < sc_cur:
                current = cand.copy()
            else:
                current = best.copy()

    rem = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    if rem > 5.0:
        # A short multiplicative peak cap polish can smooth out numerical L-BFGS residuals.
        if rem > 60.0:
            tmp = multiplicative_update(best, min(45.0, rem * 0.35), cap_factor=0.975)
            sc, _ = fast_score(tmp)
            if sc < best_score:
                best = tmp.copy()
                best_score = sc
        best = peak_refine(best, max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic()))
        sc, _ = fast_score(best)
        if sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc

    return best


def quick_template(n, budget_s, rng):
    """Run a small-length optimization to create a useful stretched template."""
    n = int(n)
    budget_s = float(max(0.0, budget_s))
    if n <= 0 or not HAVE_SCIPY or budget_s < 10.0:
        return None

    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + budget_s

    cand_list = make_candidates(n, rng)
    scored = []
    for p in cand_list:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        sc, b = fast_score(p)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((sc, p.copy()))

    if not scored:
        return None

    scored.sort(key=tuple_key0)
    best_p = scored[0][1].copy()

    top = scored[:4]
    if not top:
        return None

    per = max(5.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) / max(1, len(top)) * 0.7)
    for _sc, p in top:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 5.0:
            break
        p2 = run_lbfgs_continuation(p, n, min(per, deadline - time.monotonic()), rng)
        sc2, _ = fast_score(p2)
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < float("inf"):
            if best_p is None or sc2 < fast_score(best_p)[0]:
                best_p = p2.copy()

    return best_p


def get_actual_score(seq):
    """Call the injected evaluator, returning inf on any failure."""
    ev = globals().get("evaluate_sequence", None)
    if ev is None:
        return float("inf")
    try:
        val = float(ev(seq))
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        return float("inf")
    return val


def safe_to_list(p):
    """Convert an internal p to a valid Python list for evaluate_sequence."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    if p.ndim != 1 or p.size == 0:
        return [1.0]
    p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    p = np.minimum(p, 1000.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return [1.0]
    p = p / s
    return [float(x) for x in p]


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Main entry point.

    Returns a list of nonnegative floats minimizing the injected score.
    """
    start = time.monotonic()
    # Leave enough time for final actual-evaluator checks and return.
    deadline = start + max(60.0, float(budget_s) - 180.0)
    seed_i = int(seed)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed_i)

    answers = []
    best_p = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    def add_answer(sc, p):
        if p is None:
            return
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            answers.append((float(sc), np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).copy()))
            if len(answers) > 16:
                answers.sort(key=tuple_key0)
                answers = answers[:10]
                global_temp = eval  # no-effect placeholder to avoid syntax? remove below

    # The helper above would be nested; remove and instead do inline updates.
    # We cannot redefine nested functions, so use a top-level-free pattern:
    # (Code below updates local variables directly.)

    # --- Try provided previous construction -------------------------------------------------
    parent_raw = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if parent_raw is not None:
        try:
            p_parent = normalize_seq(np.asarray(parent_raw, dtype=np.float64))
            sc_parent, _ = fast_score(p_parent)
            if np.isfinite(sc_parent):
                best_p = p_parent.copy()
                best_score = sc_parent
                answers.append((sc_parent, p_parent.copy()))
                print("parent length=%d internal_score=%.9f" % (p_parent.size, sc_parent), flush=True)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # --- Small templates to seed larger searches ---------------------------------------------
    templates = []
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 800.0 and HAVE_SCIPY:
        t128 = quick_template(128, min(55.0, rem * 0.08), rng)
        if t128 is not None:
            templates.append(t128)
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 550.0:
            t64 = quick_template(64, min(35.0, rem * 0.07), rng)
            if t64 is not None:
                templates.append(t64)
        print("templates built: %d, time=%.1f" % (len(templates), time.monotonic() - start), flush=True)

    # --- Main searches ----------------------------------------------------------------------
    lengths_to_try = [4096]
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 900.0:
        lengths_to_try.append(8192)

    for n in lengths_to_try:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= deadline - 150.0:
            break

        print("starting length n=%d" % n, flush=True)
        cand_list = make_candidates(n, rng)

        for tmpl in templates:
            if tmpl is not None:
                push_candidate(cand_list, resample_seq(tmpl, n))

        # Also include a perturbed parent if available and resampled to this n.
        if parent_raw is not None:
            try:
                p_par_n = resample_seq(np.asarray(parent_raw, dtype=np.float64), n)
                push_candidate(cand_list, p_par_n)
                # slightly perturbed versions
                noise_scale = (0.05, 0.12, 0.25)
                for ns in noise_scale:
                    push_candidate(cand_list, p_par_n * np.exp(ns * rng.standard_normal(n)))
            except Exception:
                pass

        scored = []
        for p in cand_list:
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 150.0:
                break
            sc, b = fast_score(p)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                scored.append((sc, p.copy()))

        if not scored:
            continue

        scored.sort(key=tuple_key0)
        top_sc, top_p = scored[0]
        if top_sc < best_score:
            best_score = top_sc
            best_p = top_p.copy()
        # Keep only modest number of answers.
        for sc, p in scored[:6]:
            answers.append((sc, p.copy()))
        if len(answers) > 18:
            answers.sort(key=tuple_key0)
            answers = answers[:12]

        print("n=%d best initial=%.9f" % (n, scored[0][0]), flush=True)

        if HAVE_SCIPY:
            stage_budgets = (
                min(260.0, max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic()) - 500.0),
                min(190.0, max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic()) - 350.0),
                min(130.0, max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic()) - 250.0),
            )
            for i, (sc, p) in enumerate(scored[:3]):
                if i >= len(stage_budgets):
                    break
                bgt = float(stage_budgets[i])
                now = time.monotonic()
                if now >= deadline - 120.0:
                    break
                bgt = min(bgt, max(0.0, deadline - now - 120.0))
                if bgt < 25.0:
                    break

                print("  L-BFGS candidate %d budget=%.1fs" % (i, bgt), flush=True)
                p2 = run_lbfgs_continuation(p, n, bgt, rng)
                sc2, _ = fast_score(p2)
                if np.isfinite(sc2):
                    if sc2 < best_score:
                        best_score = sc2
                        best_p = p2.copy()
                        print("    improved score=%.9f" % sc2, flush=True)
                    answers.append((sc2, p2.copy()))
                    if len(answers) > 18:
                        answers.sort(key=tuple_key0)
                        answers = answers[:12]

        else:
            # Fallback: robust multiplicative and peak refinement.
            for sc, p in scored[:4]:
                now = time.monotonic()
                if now >= deadline - 120.0:
                    break
                bgt = min(60.0, max(0.0, deadline - now - 120.0))
                if bgt < 5.0:
                    break
                p2 = multiplicative_update(p, min(30.0, bgt), cap_factor=0.97)
                sc2, _ = fast_score(p2)
                if np.isfinite(sc2):
                    answers.append((sc2, p2.copy()))
                    if sc2 < best_score:
                        best_score = sc2
                        best_p = p2.copy()
                p3 = peak_refine(p2, max(0.0, bgt - 30.0))
                sc3, _ = fast_score(p3)
                if np.isfinite(sc3):
                    answers.append((sc3, p3.copy()))
                    if sc3 < best_score:
                        best_score = sc3
                        best_p = p3.copy()

    # --- Global polish on best sequence -----------------------------------------------------
    if best_p is not None:
        rem = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 90.0)
        if rem > 30.0:
            print("global peak_refine budget=%.1f" % rem, flush=True)
            p2 = peak_refine(best_p, min(180.0, rem))
            sc2, _ = fast_score(p2)
            if sc2 < best_score:
                best_score = sc2
                best_p = p2.copy()
            answers.append((sc2, p2.copy()))

            if HAVE_SCIPY:
                rem = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 90.0)
                if rem > 260.0:
                    n = best_p.size
                    # Another continuation from the best point, with jittered restarts.
                    for it in range(3):
                        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 100.0:
                            break
                        bgt = min(170.0, max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 100.0))
                        if bgt < 40.0:
                            break
                        p3 = best_p * np.exp(0.05 * rng.standard_normal(best_p.size))
                        p3 = normalize_seq(p3)
                        p4 = run_lbfgs_continuation(p3, n, bgt, rng)
                        sc4, _ = fast_score(p4)
                        if sc4 < best_score:
                            best_score = sc4
                            best_p = p4.copy()
                            print("restart improved score=%.9f" % sc4, flush=True)
                        answers.append((sc4, p4.copy()))
                        if len(answers) > 18:
                            answers.sort(key=tuple_key0)
                            answers = answers[:12]

    # Ensure best_p is represented.
    if best_p is not None and np.isfinite(best_score):
        answers.append((best_score, best_p))

    if not answers:
        # Guaranteed valid fallback.
        return [1.0]

    answers.sort(key=tuple_key0)

    # --- Verify with the actual evaluator for a handful of top candidates -------------------
    final_seq = None
    final_actual = float("inf")
    evaluated = 0
    for sc, p in answers:
        if evaluated >= 5 or time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        seq = safe_to_list(p)
        val = get_actual_score(seq)
        evaluated += 1
        if val < final_actual:
            final_actual = val
            final_seq = seq

    if final_seq is None or not np.isfinite(final_actual):
        final_seq = safe_to_list(best_p if best_p is not None else answers[0][1])

    # If actual verification was never possible, fallback to internal best.
    if final_seq is None or len(final_seq) == 0:
        final_seq = safe_to_list(best_p if best_p is not None else answers[0][1])

    print(
        "done: best_internal=%.9f best_actual=%.9f final_len=%d time=%.1f"
        % (float(best_score), float(final_actual), len(final_seq), time.monotonic() - start),
        flush=True,
    )
    return final_seq
```
